# Proposed additions to my_egyptian_dictionary.csv

- my dictionary: **17544** parsed entries (291 unparseable lines skipped)
- reference entries checked: **4507**
- already covered (same glyph sequence present): **1796**
- **missing** (neither glyph sequence nor meaning present): **1374**
- new writing of a meaning I already have: **1337**
- could not be transcribed to Unicode: **0**

Notes marked `~approx` contain at least one Manuel-de-Codage token that could not be resolved; `~spelled-out` means the hieroglyphs were reconstructed uniliterally from the transliteration because the source gave no writing. Review both before pasting.
